## New notes — Claude (second pass)

- **C1 — scan 480, (14) — own idea — Claude (second pass).** With σ acting by conjugation by J, σ(b) = JbJ⁻¹ =
  det(b)·ᵗb⁻¹, so on Gl(2) the condition σ(b) = εb reads b ᵗb = ε det b, not "b ᵗb = ε" (and the brace label "ᵗb⁻¹"
  holds only on SG). Counterexample: b = diag(1,−1) ∈ N′_σ(ℤ) has bσb⁻¹ = −σ, so ε = −1, but b ᵗb = 1. Confidence high.
- **C2 — scan 474, theorem (39) — own idea — Claude (second pass).** The ink cites "satisfaisant (17) et (31)", but (17)
  (p. 536, scan 467) defines SM as M[0]·SG; the conditions on (α,β,f) are (27) (det conditions) and (31) (loop
  relation) of p. 538', as scan 472 says "(27)(31)". Probably a slip for (27). Confidence medium.
- **C3 — scans 479–482, coordinate orders — unpursued implication — Claude (second pass).** On scan 479 the ambient
  (1) and the coordinates (3) were revised from (U,a,b) to (a,b,U), and (2), (4) to B′₀ × T_ρ × N′_σ × G and
  (u,a,b,U); (15), (22), (27), (29) use the revised order, while (5), (7), (13), (14) keep the older one (U first, u
  last). Formulas taken from these pages must be read in the order of their own display.
- **C4 — scans 479–480, (2) and (15) — unproved claim — Claude (second pass).** With the corrected fourth factor G
  ((2): B′₀ × T_ρ × N′_σ × G; (15): B′₀ × T_σ × N′_σ × G × G_m × μ₂ × μ₂), the embeddings are those of (42) on scan
  475; with B₀ they would be false, U = Θ_G(x) being any element of G with det U = μ. (15) also writes T_σ for T_ρ in
  second place (kept as written).
- **C5 — scan 490, the "points critiques" — own idea — Claude (second pass).** The eight points 0, 1, ∞, ½, 2, −1, −j̄,
  −j are the cusps and the points of P¹ − {0,1,∞} with non-trivial stabiliser in the anharmonic group S₃: ½, 2, −1 are
  fixed by the involutions q ↦ 1 − q, q ↦ q/(q − 1), q ↦ 1/q, and −j, −j̄ by the 3-cycles q ↦ 1/(1 − q),
  q ↦ 1 − 1/q. Confidence high.
- **C6 — scan 476, (46)–(47) — own idea — Claude (second pass).** 𝒯′ ⊂ 𝒯 is the kernel of ε₃ = det a/det b, and the
  two sections ψ_σ, ψ_ρ coincide on the diagonal of T_ρ ∩ N′_σ, which over fields is G_m but over ℤ/4 contains
  2ρ + 1 (N10). The comparison of the two scindages at 2 is therefore not determined by the fibres over fields.
  Confidence high for the example.
- **C7 — scan 484, (41)–(42) — own idea (verification) — Claude (second pass).** From ξ = αRα⁻¹R⁻¹ with α = (a b; c d),
  det α = ε′ = ±1: ξ = ε′(P + Q, −P; S + T, −S) with P = −(ac + bd + bc), Q = a² + ab + b², S = −(c² + cd + d²),
  T = ad + ac + bd. Hence ε′B = ac + bd + bc and ε′D = c² + cd + d², exactly (42) (the struck "/ε" was the same
  formula solved for D). Confidence high.
- **C8 — scan 486, "à partir du §46" — own idea — Claude (second pass).** The Circle table places §46 "Notations
  définitives (?)" at p. 390, with §46.V "Récapitulation en termes de M̃!₀,₃[0]" at p. 407: the work "dans M̃₀,₃[0]"
  began there, which supports the reading §46 (change 15). Confidence medium.


# ===== LM_C3-492-510 (checked notes) =====

# LM_C3-492-510 — research notes, checked against the corrected transcription

Each research note of the first pass appears below with its scan number, kind and verdict (keep, correct or drop), followed by the reason. Part B adds the second pass's own notes, which are labelled as such. References are to scan numbers. Quotations follow `LM_C3-492-510_checked.tex`.

## A. First-pass notes, checked

**A1. 492 — conjecture/expectation — keep.** "il faudrait … interpréter cet homomorphisme" (50), T̃_{1,1} → GL(2,Ẑ), as the fundamental "modulaire" homomorphism of the theory of elliptic curves. This is a desired interpretation, not a proof. *Reason:* the wording is confirmed on the ink.

**A2. 492 — unproved claim — keep.** The text says that the identification of the inverse image of Γ_Q ⊂ Γ̃_Q in T̃_{1,1} with T_{1,1} ≃ π_1(M_{1,1 Q}) "doit être plus ou moins tautologique, par construction". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A3. 492 — unproved claim — correct.** "Il est clair en tous cas que les deux coïncident pour tt γ ∈ M̃_{0,3}, vus (comme) éléments de" μ_2(Ẑ) = {±1}^P (51). The insertion is legible, so the claim is stated for every γ ∈ M̃_{0,3}; only the linking word after it is uncertain. *Reason:* the first pass called the domain transcription-sensitive, and the insertion settles it.

**A4. 493 — conjecture (explicitly unproved) — keep.** "C'est moralement sûr que les deux coïncident — je n'ai pas essayé de le prouver". The discrepancy is a homomorphism Γ̃_Q → μ_2(Ẑ) ≃ {±1}^P, which the text rewrites as G_m(Ẑ)_2 → μ_2(Ẑ). *Reason:* confirmed.

**A5. 493 — conjecture/expectation — keep.** (35) and (36) "doivent correspondre sûrement" to the action of Γ_Q on the Tate modules of elliptic curves "à isomorphismes exceptionnels" at the points (1/2, 2, −1) and (−j̄, −j) of M_{0,3 Q} = M_{1,1}[2]'_Q. The sign indeterminacy "doit (?) correspondre" to lifting a point of M_{1,1}[2]'_Q to M_{1,1}[2]_Q, i.e. to choosing a Legendre-rigidified curve. *Reason:* the ink reads "isomorphismes", not "automorphismes"; the inserted "doit" remains uncertain.

**A6. 493 — own idea (first pass, low) — keep.** Specialise at the two exceptional configurations, tracking the choice of curve and of level-2 rigidification. *Reason:* still a sensible test.

**A7. 494 — unproved claim — correct.** The splittings Ψ^π_{1/2} and Ψ^π_{−j̄} are splittings of M̃_{0,3} only, not of the two-sheeted extension T̃_{1,1}. Lifting them "en des scindages de T̃_{1,1} sur T_{1,1}" is said to amount exactly to choosing elliptic curves with Legendre rigidification over 1/2 and −j̄. *Reason:* the insertion reads T_{1,1}, not T_{0,3} (change 4).

**A8. 494 — unpursued implication — keep.** The loop-group computations "redonnent (sans les avoir cherchés !)" results that "collent de très près" to particularly crucial arithmetic-geometric truths. The statement is qualitative. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A9. 495 — unpursued implication — keep.** The margin calls section I "entièrement inutile pour la suite", "un tâtonnement préliminaire pour déboucher sur la section II p.553", so its results are not prerequisites of II. The author's later table names these parts "I. Préliminaires heuristiques" (p. 550) and "II. Les groupes M_{ρ,σ} etc." (p. 553). *Reason:* confirmed, and the table agrees on the page.

**A10. 495 — unproved claim — keep.** (3)–(6) follow "de sorte que" without proof: G[0] = Norm_G(L_0^C), G = G(0)·C^{+∧}_{0,3} (semi-direct), G[0] = G(0)·L_0^C and G[0] ∩ C^{+∧}_{0,3} = L_0^C. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A11. 496 — unpursued implication — keep.** Under the surjection φ, only the inclusions (10)–(11) are stated; equalities would require lifting normalising elements. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A12. 496 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Isolate the kernel condition under which α and β descend from G[0] to M[0]. *Reason:* scan 497, (18)–(20), carries this out, so the note stands as a pointer.

**A13. 497 — unpursued implication — correct.** With T_ρ = Centr_M(ρ) and T_σ = Centr_M(σ) (17), "modulo T_ρ resp. T_σ, α, β ne dépendent que de u". The following "Hypothèse" asks for strict independence, which is more. *Reason:* the notation is plain T, not T̄ (change 5).

**A14. 497 — unproved claim — keep.** The hypothesis is rewritten as α^π(δ_0), β^π(δ_0) ∈ Ker φ for all δ_0 ∈ J = Ker(G[0] → M[0]), using the cocycle formulas α'_0 = δ_0(α_0)ᾱ_0 and β'_0 = δ_0(β_0)β̄_0. *Reason:* confirmed; the superscript is still π or Π.

**A15. 497 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Test the descent on topological generators of J. *Reason:* unchanged.

**A16. 498 — unproved claim — keep.** (26), ζ = η ε_1^ν with ν = (μ−1)/2, "provient de la relation unique dans C^+_{0,3}". No convention is needed for ν ∈ Ẑ: μ ∈ Ẑ^* is odd at 2, and 2 is a unit at the odd primes. *Reason:* confirmed; remark on the exponent added.

**A17. 498 — unpursued implication — keep.** (27), the factorisation of the multiplier χ^G through M, is a simplifying assumption made "pour fixer les idées". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A18. 498 — unproved claim — keep.** The text says the action M[0] → Aut(C) is known once α and β are known, and even once ζ and η alone are. The reason is given in the margin of 499: "car ρ, σ engendrent topolog^t C". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A19. 499 — question — keep.** "il n'est pas clair qu'il en soit de m̄ de α, β, quand M[0] n'agit (n'opère?) pas fidèlement sur C". *Reason:* confirmed; the verb is uncertain.

**A20. 499 — unproved claim — keep.** (30), η^{-1}ζ ∈ L_1, "implique (26) (avec la forme précise de l'exposant ν)" if C is large enough for C^{+∧}_{0,3} → SL(2,Ẑ) (or PSL) to factor through C. The insertion also has a "2" under the arrow that is not explained. *Reason:* confirmed; the "2" is now transcribed (change 11).

**A21. 499 — unpursued implication — keep.** The factorisation is assumed "pour nous simplifier la vie". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A22. 499 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Look for examples with a nontrivial centraliser that separate α, β from ζ, η. *Reason:* unchanged.

**A23. 500 — unpursued implication — keep.** The margin note says that only σ^{-1}ρ = ε_0 and the fact that ε_0 generates L_0 are used, "et pas que σ^2 = 1 ou ρ^3 = 1 p.ex.". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A24. 500 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** From u(ε_0) ∈ L_0 one gets only u(L_0) ⊂ L_0; the equality u(L_0) = L_0 written next requires u(ε_0) to generate L_0. The earlier step, from σ(u(ε_0)ε_0^{-1}) ∈ L_1 to u(ε_0)ε_0^{-1} ∈ L_0, is justified on the page by "(puisque L_0 = σ^{-1}(L_1))". *Reason:* kept; the restored "puisque" (change 13) shows that the gap lies only in the equality.

**A25. 500 — unproved claim — keep.** (36) is "injective parce que ρ, σ engendrent C". In the profinite case this needs topological generation and continuous automorphisms. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A26. 501 — explicit hypothesis — keep.** (37) b), "L'application (36) (injective par a)) est bijective", is part of the "Hypothèse fondamentale"; it is an assumption, not a theorem. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A27. 501 — unpursued implication — keep.** The reformulation in terms of α and β reads "il existe u ∈ Aut(C) tel qu'on ait …; (on aura donc nécessairement u ∈ B'_0, et u sera unique si on a a))". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A28. 501 — claim (no longer "left for later") — correct.** The page reads "on aura une structure de groupe, (+/et) déduite par transport de structure de celle de B̃'_0". The group law on R_{ρ,σ} is the one transported from B̃'_0 through (36); nothing is deferred, and the law is written out on 504, (13). *Reason:* the first pass misread "déduite" as "à décrire" (change 14).

**A29. 501 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Count admissible pairs in finite quotients to test (37) b). *Reason:* unchanged.

**A30. 502 — unpursued implication — keep.** The margin sends the reader to a systematic rectification of the notation, "cf p. 564, remarque(s)". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A31. 502 — unproved claim — keep.** (6) states that u normalises L_0 if and only if η^{-1}ζ ∈ L_1, if and only if ζ = ηε_1^ν. The argument on 500 gives only the direction leading to u(L_0) ⊂ L_0 (see A24). *Reason:* confirmed.

**A32. 503 — hypothesis — keep.** (10): a) ρ and σ generate C topologically; b) the map (9) "(injective par a)) est surjective (donc bijective)". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A33. 503 — unproved claim — keep.** The margin reads "Attention, cette condition est [pas cancelled] satisfaite pour C = SL(2,Ẑ)' !", followed by a parenthesis on ε = ε_3(μ). With "pas" cancelled the claim is positive; the parenthesis is still largely unread. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A34. 503 — own idea (first pass) — keep, refined.** The continuity of φ^{-1} comes for free once φ is continuous for compact Hausdorff topologies on both sides. The real question is which topology Aut(C) carries, for example that of a topologically finitely generated profinite group. *Reason:* refined; the first pass's "high confidence" is lowered to medium.

**A35. 504 — unproved claim — keep.** (13), the law on Σ: ζ'' = u_{ζ',η'}(ζ)ζ' and η'' = u_{ζ',η'}(η)η'. It is checked in B1. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A36. 504 — unpursued implication — keep.** The group G_{ρ,σ} is required to contain C as an invariant subgroup and to receive B_{ρ,σ}, whose image B'_{ρ,σ} contains N_0, so as to realise the tautological action. The page has the construction inside Aut(C) when the centre is trivial and a cancelled semidirect-product attempt. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A37. 504 — conjecture/expectation — keep.** "Il me semble que l'introduction de N_0 (sur lequel en pratique nous n'avons aucun contrôle) n'était pas non plus judicieuse" (finished on 505). The condition B'_{ρ,σ} ⊃ N_0 is then replaced by B'_{ρ,σ} ⊃ L_0. *Reason:* sentence completed with the corrected reading "n'était" (change 22).

**A38. 504 — unproved claim — keep.** Triviality of the centre "n'est pas satisfaite si C = SL(2,Ẑ)/±1". *Reason:* confirmed; "soit" or "est" trivial.

**A39. 505 — unproved claim — correct.** The image of i: L_0 → S_0G_{ρ,σ}, g ↦ int(g)·g^{-1}, is "un sous-groupe distingué", because a) it commutes with C and b) it is invariant under B_{ρ,σ}; then G_{ρ,σ} = S_0G_{ρ,σ}/i(L_0) (15). That i(L_0) is closed, as the profinite quotient requires, is not stated. *Reason:* "distingué" is retraced, not cancelled (change 23), so normality is asserted.

**A40. 505 — unpursued implication — keep.** (18), Ker(C → G_{ρ,σ}) = L_0 ∩ Centr(C) = Ker(L_0 → B_{ρ,σ}), is a narrower obstruction than the whole centre. It is checked in B2. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A41. 505 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Characterise the construction by a universal property. *Reason:* unchanged.

**A42. 506 — conjecture/expectation — keep.** "il ne semble pas à priori qu'il y ait des inconvénients majeurs à supposer" L_0 ∩ Centr(C) = {1} (18). *Reason:* confirmed.

**A43. 506 — unproved claim — keep.** (19)–(20) and the margin diagram "(20bis) i.e. hom. de suites exactes", which writes C = SG_{ρ,σ} under the second row. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A44. 506 — unproved claim — keep.** NB: for C = SL(2,Ẑ)' etc., B_{ρ,σ} ⊂ GP(Ẑ) is B'_0 (or rather its image B̃'_0); dét induces Γ_{ρ,σ} ⥲ G_m (21), and G_{ρ,σ} = GL(2,Ẑ)' (22). *Reason:* confirmed, with "etc." read.

**A45. 506 — unpursued implication — correct.** The margin reads "Dans le cas où on part de SL(2,Ẑ), ρ, σ, on tombe sur G = GL(2,Ẑ)…". The comparison is with unprimed SL(2,Ẑ) and its two generators only. *Reason:* there is no third, uncertain generator (change 25).

**A46. 506 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** A pushout-like universal property, tested on the determinant model. *Reason:* unchanged.

**A47. 507 — conjecture/expectation — keep.** In the general case Γ_{ρ,σ} need not be small or commutative; in the universal case (23)–(24), Γ_{ρ,σ} ≃ Γ̃'_{0,3} (⊃ Γ'_Q !). *Reason:* confirmed.

**A48. 507 — unproved claim/expectation — correct.** "On doit pouvoir sans inconvénient postuler l'existence d'un scindage canonique". If the quotient C of C^{+∧}_{0,3} is large enough "pour s'envoyer dans GP(Ẑ)", then B_{ρ,σ} ≃ Σ_{ρ,σ} maps to B̃'_{0(,3)}, and the inverse image of T̃_0 gives the section T_{ρ,σ} (25). *Reason:* the target group reads GP(Ẑ), the group of the NB on 506 (change 26), not "CP_2Ẑ)".

**A49. 507 — unpursued implication — keep.** T_{ρ,σ} is a section both for B → Γ and for G → Γ, which gives (26): B ≃ T·L_0 and G ≃ T·C, both semi-direct. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A50. 507 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Compare the sections obtained from two admissible target quotients. *Reason:* unchanged.

**A51. 508 — unproved claim — keep.** (28) is a "surjection par définition de Σ", and "par l'hypothèse (10) on a presque une section canonique …, à cela près que u_{ζ,η} n'est pas dans C, mais seulement dans G_{ρ,σ}". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A52. 508 — unpursued implication — keep.** The larger set g_{ρ,σ} ⊂ G × G (29) satisfies Sg_{ρ,σ} = g_{ρ,σ} ∩ (C × C) (30), "en supposant (18) pour simplifier". *Reason:* confirmed.

**A53. 508 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Separate the existence of a representative pair from the existence of a preferred automorphism. *Reason:* unchanged.

**A54. 509 — unproved claim — keep.** (32), the "section canonique" (ζ, η) ↦ (u_{ζ,η}, u_{ζ,η}); "on trouve … du §48 III)". In the author's table §48 III treats this same equation. *Reason:* the reference is now read without doubt (change 28).

**A55. 509 — unproved claim — keep.** Theorem (33)–(34): (u, a, b) ↦ (ua^{-1}, ub^{-1}) is a bijection onto g_{ρ,σ}. The ink writes B × T_ρ × T_σ in (34), although (33) defines Z_ρ and Z_σ. A proof sketch is in B4. *Reason:* confirmed; the inconsistency between T and Z is the author's.

**A56. 509 — unproved claim — keep.** NB: for C = SL(2,Ẑ)' etc., Z_ρ = T_ρ and Z_σ = N'_σ. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A57. 509 — unproved claim — keep.** The margin argues that by (7) the maps Z_ρ → B and Z_σ → B are "épi", hence so are their composites with B → Γ, and hence the maps (35). *Reason:* confirmed.

**A58. 509 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Build the inverse of (34) from the unique u attached to a pair. *Reason:* carried out in B4.

**A59. 510 — unproved claim — keep.** (36)–(38): the fibre-product condition δ_G(u) = δ_ρ(a) = δ_σ(b) and the bijection onto S_0g_{ρ,σ}. The margin traces the notation S_0Γ^b back to "§48, IX ff". *Reason:* the reference is corrected from IV to IX (change 29); see B5.

**A60. 510 — unproved claim — keep.** Scholium: the bijections transport the natural group structures, and (39) gives α'' = u'(α)α' and β'' = u'(β)β'. The defining formulas on the right are unprimed. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A61. 510 — unproved claim — keep.** The added (39)–(40): L_0 → SΓ'_{ρ,σ}, ℓ ↦ (ℓ, 1, 1), and the exact sequence 1 → L_0 → SΓ'_{ρ,σ} → Γ_{ρ,σ} → 1. Their cancellation status is uncertain. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A62. 510 — unpursued implication — keep.** The cancelled definition of M_{ρ,σ} and the cancelled (41)–(42) show a discarded presentation. *Reason:* confirmed.

**A63. 510 — unpursued implication — correct.** NB: the letter S here follows the usage (of Ahlfors?) for "groupes de Teichmüller spéciaux". It does not have "le même sens" (m̄) as in SZ_ρ, SZ_σ, where it denotes the kernels of δ_ρ, δ_σ, which play the role of a "déterminant". *Reason:* the first pass's "=" is the abbreviation m̄ (change 30).

**A64. 510 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Check the restricted bijection at the level of sets first. *Reason:* done in B5.

No first-pass note is dropped.

## B. Second-pass notes (own)

**B1. 501/504 — own idea (verification), confidence high.** For u = u_{ζ,η} and u' = u_{ζ',η'}, (u'∘u)(ρ) = u'(ζρ) = u'(ζ)u'(ρ) = u'(ζ)ζ'ρ, so ζ'' = u'(ζ)ζ'; in the same way η'' = u'(η)η'. This is (13) on 504, so the law on Σ is the transported composition law and not the componentwise one. That fits the corrected "déduite par transport de structure" on 501.

**B2. 505 — own idea (verification), confidence high.** In B ⋉ C, where int(h) acts on C by c ↦ hch^{-1}, the element i(g) = int(g)·g^{-1} centralises C, and i(g)i(h) = int(g)int(h)·(h^{-1}g^{-1}h)h^{-1} = i(gh); so i is a homomorphism. An element c ∈ C lies in i(L_0) exactly when c = g^{-1} for some g ∈ L_0 with int(g) = 1. This proves (18): Ker(C → G_{ρ,σ}) = L_0 ∩ Centr(C) = Ker(L_0 → B_{ρ,σ}).

**B3. 509 — own idea (author's slip), confidence high.** In (31) the ink has α(ρ)ρ^{+1}, while the same line gives αρ(α)^{-1} = [α, ρ], which equals α(ρ)ρ^{-1}, as in 500 (34) and 502 (5). The "+" is a slip or a malformed minus. The transcription keeps it as `\unsure{+1|-1}`.

**B4. 509 — own idea (proof sketch for (34)), confidence medium.** For a ∈ Z_ρ, [ua^{-1}, ρ] = ua^{-1}ρau^{-1}ρ^{-1} = [u, ρ], and likewise for b ∈ Z_σ; hence (ua^{-1}, ub^{-1}) ∈ g_{ρ,σ} for u ∈ B_{ρ,σ}. Conversely, given (α, β) ∈ g, take u = u_{ζ,η} with ζ = [α, ρ] and η = [β, σ] (hypothesis (10)). Then a = α^{-1}u ∈ Z_ρ and b = β^{-1}u ∈ Z_σ, which inverts (34), with uniqueness from (10) a). The argument needs Z_ρ and Z_σ as defined in (33), not the T_ρ and T_σ written in (34).

**B5. 510 — own idea (verification), confidence medium.** Since C = Ker δ_G, δ_G(ua^{-1}) = δ_B(u)δ_ρ(a)^{-1}, which is trivial exactly when δ_B(u) = δ_ρ(a), and likewise for ub^{-1}. So (34) maps the set of (36) onto g_{ρ,σ} ∩ (C × C) = Sg_{ρ,σ} (30). Hence (38) is the restriction of (34), and its target "S_0g_{ρ,σ} (défini dans (27))" agrees with (30) under (18).

**B6. 492/493 — own idea, confidence low.** The residual sign is a homomorphism Γ̃_Q → μ_2(Ẑ) = {±1}^P, which the text factors through G_m(Ẑ)_2 = (Ẑ^*)_2 ≃ {±1}^P → μ_2(Ẑ). Evaluating on complex conjugation (χ = −1) and on Frobenius-type elements would show whether this map is trivial, the identity, or a "diagonal" sign. Grothendieck's "moralement sûr" is that it is trivial.

**B7. 494 — own idea, confidence medium.** With the insertion read "sur T_{1,1}", the lifting problem concerns splittings of the two-sheeted T̃_{1,1} over T_{1,1}, on the M_{1,1} side. There a choice of an elliptic curve with Legendre rigidification over 1/2 and −j̄ is exactly the kind of datum needed. The passage thus separates the M_{0,3} side, where the Ψ's are splittings, from the M_{1,1} side, where the lifts are choices of curves.

**B8. 498/502 — own idea, confidence medium.** The looped letter before the exponent in (26) is the letter used in the 502 margin for ℓ'_0, ℓ'_1, ℓ'_∞. The relation there checks: with ε_0 = ℓ'_0, σ = ℓ'_1 and ρ^{-1} = ℓ'_∞, ℓ'_∞ℓ'_1ℓ'_0 = ρ^{-1}σσ^{-1}ρ = 1. Whatever the letter, (30) restates (26) as η^{-1}ζ ∈ L_1, so the factor must be the generator ε_1 of L_1. The transcription keeps `\unsure{\varepsilon_1|\ell_1}`.

**B9. 499 — question, confidence low.** The "2" under the arrow C^{+∧}_{0,3} → SL(2,Ẑ) may mark a level-2 condition: on 493 M_{0,3} = M_{1,1}[2]', so the factorisation may be meant through the image at level 2. This should be compared with §48 before any use.

**B10. 503/506 — unpursued implication, confidence medium.** The margin on 503 asserts that (10) b) holds for C = SL(2,Ẑ)'. Scan 506 uses exactly this to identify B_{ρ,σ} with B'_0 and to obtain G_{ρ,σ} = GL(2,Ẑ)'. The proof of this bijectivity is not on these pages; its parenthesis on ε = ε_3(μ) suggests that the mod-3 sign enters.


# ===== LM_C3-511-529 (checked notes) =====

# LM_C3-511-529 — research notes, checked against the corrected text

Scans 511–529 (ms. pp. 558–567, §49.II–III). Each first-pass note (R01–R56) is kept, corrected or dropped against `LM_C3-511-529_checked.tex`; the reason follows the note. Notes C1–C5 at the end are new, written in the second pass. Kinds: question (the author's), claim without proof, implication not pursued, own idea (of the first-pass model, or of the second pass where so labelled).

## First-pass notes

**R01 · scan 511 · claim without proof · corrected.** «on trouve que (39) induit une bijection canonique» Γ⁰_ρ,σ ⥲ L₀\S𝒢_ρ,σ. Checking it needs the map (39), its compatibility with the left translation (41), and that its fibres are exactly the L₀-orbits. *Reason:* the ink reads (39), not "38 or 39".

**R02 · scan 511 · implication not pursued · corrected.** The second line of (44) is written SZ_ρ = Ker(Z_ρ − Γ_ρ,2) = Centr_𝒢(σ): the left side has ρ twice where Centr_𝒢(σ), (45) and the restatement on scan 513 (SZ_σ = Z_σ ∩ 𝒢) have σ. Read with σ, (44) gives the two sequences (45), from which (46)–(47) follow with (37) and (42). *Reason:* the indices are now settled on the ink (ρ, an authorial slip), so the note no longer has to wait for them.

**R03 · scan 511 · claim without proof · corrected.** «on retrouve les S₀Γ⁰_ρ,σ et Γ⁰_ρ,σ de §48 IX» for 𝒢 = Sl(2,Ẑ) and a modular pair: no isomorphism is written, and the check needs the definitions of §48.IX ("Les groupes M_ρ,σ et SM_ρ,σ", p. 490). *Reason:* the reference is §48.IX, not "§48 II"; the margin of (42) gives the same reference.

**R04 · scan 512 · claim without proof · keep.** The kernel of SM⁰ → M⁰ is computed (the (u,1,1,1) with δ_B(u) = 1, i.e. L₀), but exactness on the right in (52) needs every (a,b,U) to lift, i.e. δ_B onto γ_ρ,σ. *Reason:* unaffected by the corrections; scan 513 states surjectivity only for δ_ρ, δ_σ, δ_G.

**R05 · scan 512 · implication not pursued · keep.** The projections Θ_G, Θ_ρ, Θ_σ (53) and Θ_B (54) give access to the fibre product by components; which stay surjective under weaker lifting hypotheses is not addressed. *Reason:* consistent with the text.

**R06 · scan 513 · claim without proof · keep.** (55) S₀M⁰ ≃ M⁰ ×_γ B and «l'extension de M par L₀ est l'image inverse de l'extension B de γ par L₀»: to be checked with the kernel inclusions and quotient maps, not only as an isomorphism of groups. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R07 · scan 513 · claim without proof · keep.** (57) is deduced from (56) «comme δ_ρ, δ_σ, δ_G sont épi». *Reason:* unchanged; see C1 for which kernel SZ_ρ must denote here.

**R08 · scan 513 · implication not pursued · keep.** i_𝒢 : U ↦ (1,1,U) singles out 𝒢 in M⁰; its normality and quotient are taken up in (52) on scan 514. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R11 · scan 514 · own idea (first pass) · keep.** Transporting the group law through (55) may give a twisted product rather than the direct one, as the coordinates ua⁻¹, ub⁻¹, uU⁻¹ suggest. *Reason:* still a sound proposal.

**R12 · scan 514 · implication not pursued · corrected.** In (58), ζ = αρ(α)⁻¹ and η = βσ(β)⁻¹, and g = f⁻¹ζρ(f), h = f⁻¹ησ(f) are their conjugates by f⁻¹, a change of trivialisation. *Reason:* the letter is ζ on the ink (the ζ, η of §48.II), not ξ.

**R13 · scan 515 · implication not pursued · keep.** «on n'a pas vraiment utilisé la soi-disante "hyp. fondamentale" (20), sauf la partie a)»: which steps used b) remains to be traced. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R41 · scan 524 · own idea (first pass) · keep.** Compare the pull-back extension on finite quotients with the {±1} kernel explicit. *Reason:* still a sound proposal.

**R43 · scan 525 · claim without proof · keep.** (7) M̃_{0,3} ≃ M̃_{0,3}(0)·𝒢^{+∧}_{0,3} (semi-direct) and the iso. can. obtained by restricting (2) to M̃_{0,3}(0). *Reason:* unchanged.

**R44 · scan 525 · own idea (first pass) · keep.** Keep the kernel of Sl(2,ℤ)^ → Sl(2,Ẑ) in view. *Reason:* still a sound proposal.

**R45 · scan 526 · claim without proof · keep.** The action of Ñ_{1,1}(0) ≃ Γ̃_ℚ on Sl(2,ℤ)^ «que je voudrais expliciter», via the cartesian square (10). *Reason:* unchanged.

**R46 · scan 526 · implication not pursued · keep.** Separate what holds for Sl(2,ℤ)^ from what holds for Sl(2,Ẑ). *Reason:* unchanged.

**R47 · scan 526 · own idea (first pass) · keep.** Test the lifted action on finite quotients, tracking the sign. *Reason:* still a sound proposal.

**R48 · scan 527 · claim without proof · keep.** (14)–(19): the action with the multiplier μ = χ(u) and the sign ε = ε₂(μ). *Reason:* unchanged; the doubts in (16) remain.

**R49 · scan 527 · implication not pursued · keep.** Margin: α̲, β̲ determined only up to sign, ζ̲, η̲ unambiguous; a lemma on independence of the sign is implied. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R50 · scan 528 · question · keep.** «essayer de trouver un autom. (or endom.) u_𝒢 de 𝒢 satisfaisant les relations idoines (23)» is an explicit open problem: the prescribed images of ρ_𝒢, σ_𝒢 must respect all relations of 𝒢. *Reason:* unchanged; the inserted word stays doubtful.

**R51 · scan 528 · own idea (first pass) · keep.** «élément central canonique involutif, noté −1»: by (22), (ρ³)² = ρ⁶ = 1, so "involutif" means square 1; the element may be trivial in 𝒢. *Reason:* completed with the computation.

**R52 · scan 528 · claim without proof · keep.** «Notons aussi» (20) u(ε₀) = ε₀^μ, without computation. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R53 · scan 529 · implication not pursued · keep.** Margin «ce qui n'est pas la convention habituelle jusqu'à présent…»: (24) changes the convention for ε₀, ε₁. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R54 · scan 529 · claim without proof · corrected (checked in the second pass).** From (24) ε₀ = σρ, ε₁ = ρσ and (25): τ(ε₀) = σ⁻¹·σρ⁻¹σ⁻¹ = (σρ)⁻¹ = ε₀⁻¹, and τ(ε₁) = σρ⁻¹σ⁻², which equals ε₁⁻¹ = σ⁻¹ρ⁻¹ exactly when σ² = ρ³, i.e. by (22). Moreover τ² is the identity on ρ and σ, and τ respects ρ³ = σ² and ρ⁶ = σ⁴ = 1, so {1,τ} acts on the group presented by (22). What remains open is that τ preserves the kernel of Sl(2,ℤ)^ → 𝒢. *Reason:* the first pass asked for these checks; they hold.

**R55 · scan 529 · claim without proof · keep.** «paraphraser des constructions déjà faites par ailleurs»: the constructions to be transferred to 𝒢^• are not identified on this page. *Reason:* unchanged.

**R56 · scan 529 · own idea (first pass) · keep.** Separate the action on the presented group from the preservation of Ker(Sl(2,ℤ)^ → 𝒢). *Reason:* still a sound proposal; R54 settles the first half.

## New notes (Claude, second pass)

**C1 · scans 511, 513 · implication not pursued (Claude).** SZ_ρ is defined twice: Ker(Z_ρ → Γ_ρ,σ) in (44), scan 511, and Z_ρ ∩ 𝒢 on scan 513, which is Ker(Z_ρ → γ_ρ,σ) because 𝒢 = SG_ρ,σ = Ker(G_ρ,σ → γ_ρ,σ) (margin of 511). The two agree only where Γ_ρ,σ → γ_ρ,σ is injective on the image of Z_ρ, i.e. where the margin's SΓ_ρ,σ = Ker(Γ_ρ,σ → γ_ρ,σ) meets it trivially; (46) and (57) need the second description. The quotient written Γ_ρ,σ in (50) on scan 513 (where (57) has γ_ρ,σ) is right only in that case; the margin (92) on scan 522 records SΓ′ = 1 in the universal case.

**C2 · scan 512 · claim without proof (Claude).** «redonnant les hom analogues sur SM⁰_ρ,σ par composition avec (41)»: the maps Θ on SM⁰ come from composing with the projection (51) SM⁰ → M⁰, whereas (41) is the L₀-action on S𝒢_ρ,σ. (41) is probably a slip for (51); the transcription keeps (41).

**C3 · scan 516 · implication not pursued (Claude).** In (63) the index of M̃ is overwritten (ρ,σ → 0,3, reading doubtful): 1 → 𝒢^{+∧}_{0,3} → M̃_{0,3} → Γ̃_{0,3} → 1 is the sequence that (64)–(65) use, while «revenons à M̃_ρ,σ» just above was left as it was. From here on the objects are M̃_{0,3} and its subgroup M′̃_{0,3}, as the Théorème on scan 518 confirms.

**C4 · scan 517 · claim without proof (Claude).** The definition of "effectif" reads (ζ_𝒢, η_𝒢) = (u(ζ), u(η)) on the ink, but for (69) u_𝒢(ρ_𝒢) = ζ_𝒢ρ_𝒢 to make sense in 𝒢, ζ_𝒢 and η_𝒢 must be ψ(ζ) and ψ(η). The definition has to be read with ψ; the first pass's alternative "ψ" gave the sense, not the ink.

**C5 · scan 523 · own idea (Claude), a check.** In (95) B′ has μ ≡ 1 (3) and G′_ρ,σ = Gl′(2,Ẑ) = {det ≡ 1 (3)}, but T′_σ = {tσ + u | t² + u² ∈ Ẑ*} carries no condition mod 3: det(tσ + u) = t² + u² can be ≡ 2 (mod 3) (t = 1 and u ∈ Ẑ with u ≡ 2 mod 3, u ≡ 0 mod 2 and mod every p ≥ 5), and det τ_∞ = −1. So T′_σ·{1,τ_∞} is not contained in G′_ρ,σ; its elements of determinant ≡ 1 (mod 3) form a subgroup of index 2. For Z′_ρ no condition is needed, since c² + cd + d² ≡ (c − d)² ≡ 1 (mod 3) for a unit; whether Z′_σ was meant inside Gl′(2,Ẑ) or inside Gl(2,Ẑ) is open.

conjectures, questions, expectations, plans), unproved claim, unpursued implication, own idea. "Unproved" means not
proved at the cited place. Conventions of the pages: x(y) = xyx⁻¹, [x,y] = x y(x)⁻¹, −1 = σ² = ρ³. Totals: 50 kept,
12 corrected, 1 dropped, 10 new.

  548. The margin's equivalence with a surjection SL(2,Z)^∧ → 𝒢 needs ρ, σ to generate 𝒢 topologically, which the

## Claude (second pass)

- **C1 — scan 530, (1)–(5) — own idea (check).** (2) implies (3) and (4): τ² fixes σ, and τ²(ρ) = σ⁻¹(σρ⁻¹σ⁻¹)⁻¹σ = ρ;
  τ(σρ) = σ⁻¹σρ⁻¹σ⁻¹ = (σρ)⁻¹. With σ = (0 −1; 1 0), ρ = (0 1; −1 1): ε₀ = σρ = −σ⁻¹ρ = (1 −1; 0 1), ε₁ = ρσ = σ(ε₀)
  = σ⁻¹(ε₀) = ρ(ε₀). The margin's "signe −" is the sign in ε₀ = −σ⁻¹ρ, which 533 removes with ε′₀ = σ⁻¹ρ.
- **C2 — scan 532, (12) — unpursued implication.** If the unstruck insertions are live, (12) reads α ∈ 𝒢 ⟺ μ ≡ 1 (3),
  the form used in (A) on 535; it is the coset statement α ≡ τ^{ν₃(μ)} (𝒢) of the second (17) on 534.
- **C3 — scans 533–534, (16) — own idea (check).** From u(ε′₀) = ε′₀^μ, u(ρ) = ζρ, u(σ) = ησ: σ⁻¹η⁻¹ζρ = (σ⁻¹ρ)^μ,
  hence η⁻¹ζ = (ρσ⁻¹)^{μ−1} = ε′₁^{2ν}, and ε′₁^{2ν} = ε₁^{2ν} = l₁^ν since −1 is central. This is the "sempiternel
  calcul"; it uses only that μ is odd.
- **C4 — scan 534, lemma — unproved claim.** The last step int(α)(−1) = −1 uses that −1 = σ² = ρ³ is central in 𝒢,
  which holds because ρ, σ generate 𝒢 topologically; the proof leaves this implicit.
- **C5 — scans 538–539 — own idea (check).** Both obstructions are complete. ρ and σ are cyclic (companion) matrices,
  so their centralizers in M₂(R) are R[ρ], R[σ], and the conjugators are fτ′∞, fτ∞ with f in them. det(cρ + d) =
  c² + cd + d² takes only the values 0, 1 mod 3; det(tσ + u) = t² + u² only 0, 1, 2 mod 4; −1 is never reached. With
  the traces 1 and −1 of ρ and −ρ⁻¹, ρ is conjugate to neither ρ⁻¹ nor −ρ⁻¹ in SL(2,Z/3Z).
- **C6 — scans 538 and 541, τ′∞ — own idea (check).** τ∞σ∞ = σ∞⁻¹τ∞ = −σ∞τ∞ = (0 1; 1 0), of determinant −1, and
  conjugation by it sends ρ∞ to ρ∞⁻¹ = (1 −1; 1 0) and σ∞ to σ∞⁻¹: this is (40), the "(τσ)(ρ) = ρ⁻¹" of 538 and (*) on
  548.
- **C7 — scan 541, case ρ = −1 — own idea (check).** The abelianization of (1) is Z/12Z with ρ ↦ 2, σ ↦ 3, the author's
  first version. If ρ = −1 = σ², 𝒢 is generated by σ with σ⁴ = 1, so it is a quotient of Z/4Z with σ ↦ 3 = −1 and
  ρ ↦ 2, as the inserted correction says.
- **C8 — scans 542–543, (48) and (50) — own idea.** δ_ρ is applied there to elements outside Z_ρ, so it stands for δ_G,
  whose kernel is 𝒢. Since σ ∈ 𝒢, δ_G(ατ^{ν₃}) = δ_G(ατ′^{ν₃}): the change from τ in (48) to τ′ in (50) is harmless.
  The two conditions say β ∈ 𝒢 and ατ^{ν₃(μ)} ∈ 𝒢, i.e. the second (17).
- **C9 — scan 544, (53)–(58) — own idea (consistency).** (54) writes β = u·i_σ(b) where (53) gives ub⁻¹. For the set this
  is harmless, since b ↦ b⁻¹ preserves N_σ and ε_σ. But (56) "u·i_σ(b) ∈ 𝒢" gives δ_G(u) = δ_σ(b)⁻¹, while (57) has
  δ_G(u) = δ_σ(b), which holds for β = ub⁻¹, the form of (53) and (58). So the slip sits in (54) and (56).
- **C10 — scan 545, (60) — unproved claim.** Under (u,a,b) the ink puts b ∈ Z_σ, but then ε_σ(b) = 1 and the condition
  ε_σ(b) = ε₂(χ_γ(Δ)) would force ε₂ = 1. (55), (58) and (61) have b ∈ N_σ, so Z_σ here is a slip for N_σ.


# ===== LM_C3-549-567 (checked notes) =====

# LM_C3-549-567: research notes checked against the corrected transcription (scans 549–567)

**Source:** the first pass's research notes in `answers/unzipped/LM_C3-549-567/LM_C3-549-567_notes.md` (§ "Research
notes", 9 + 13 + 12 + 13 = 47 notes, numbered here N1–N47 in their order). Each note was checked against
`LM_C3-549-567_checked.tex`.

**Verdicts:**
- keep: the note stands as written.
- correct: the note is revised; the reason is given.
- drop: the note is withdrawn.

**Kinds:**
- conjecture: Grothendieck's conjectures, questions and expectations.
- unproved claim: a claim without proof in the cited passage.
- unpursued implication.
- own idea.

Notes marked **Claude (second pass)** are new.

These pages belong to §49 V, "Les groupes M_{ρ,σ} généraux (contexte profini)", upper numbers 578–587. Scan 549 is the
verso 577′ that scan 566 cites for (6). The changes table is `LM_C3-549-567_changes.md`.

## Notes of the first pass

### Scans 549–553

- **N1 — scan 549 — unproved claim — keep, corrected.** In (6) the sign is ⊂ (changes, row 3), so "C'est un
  sous-groupe du groupe produit" is a claim that the defining conditions are stable. The check is short. If u(ε₀)=ε₀^μ
  and v(ε₀)=ε₀^ν, then uv(ε₀)=ε₀^{μν}, and 𝒢⁺-conjugacy to ρ^{ε′} and to ρ^{η′} composes to conjugacy to ρ^{ε′η′}
  (likewise for σ). Profinite exponents need u to be continuous.
- **N2 — scan 550 — unproved claim — keep.** "Si 𝒢 est 'assez gros'": the size condition is not made precise. The
  modular case (12) is the model in which {±1} ↪ 𝒢⁺ and L_ρ∩L_σ = {±1} hold.
- **N3 — scan 550 — unpursued implication — keep.** (10) follows from (6) because inversion preserves the cyclic
  subgroups L_ρ and L_σ. The converse is not claimed.
- **N4 — scan 550 — unproved claim — keep.** The text reads "est injectif, donc μ est connu" (row 5), which does not
  change the note. The two uniqueness checks (the signs from non-conjugacy, μ from the injectivity of (11)) remain
  separate.
- **N5 — scan 551 — unproved claim — keep, corrected.** Both signs in (14) are inclusions on the image. The argument
  "ℓ = ρ(ℓ) ∈ ρ(L₀) = L₁" gives only L₀∩Cent_{𝒢⁺}(ρ) ⊂ L₀∩L₁. The reverse inclusion is not claimed. "Ce qui est le cas
  si (12) existe" needs L₀∩L₁ = {1} in the image in Gl(2,Ẑ) (or Gl(2,Ẑ)′ for (12′)), and this is not proved on the
  page.
- **N6 — scans 551, 553 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Keeping K = L₀∩Cent(𝒢⁺) visible instead of assuming (15)
  is a sound reformulation. The parenthesis after (26) identifies both kernels with K.
- **N7 — scan 552 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** The image of (χ_γ, ε_γ, ε′_γ) in Ẑ*×{±1}×{±1} is not determined
  on these pages.
- **N8 — scan 553 — unproved claim — keep.** "Dont l'image est encore invariante" (23) is asserted without proof.
  The sentence after it reads "On pose" over struck words (row 8).
- **N9 — scan 553 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Reading (26)–(27) as a change of kernel for the extension of
  γ_{ρ,σ} is a fair suggestion, provided the universal property is stated and proved.

### Scans 554–558

- **N10 — scan 554 — unproved claim — correct.** (31) defines i_𝒢 by its value on τ: the prime of τ′ is struck
  (row 11), with τ_Z = (int(τ)|𝒢⁺, −1, −1, −1). Extending it to an embedding needs τ_Z ∈ Z_{ρ,σ}, which the margin
  asserts ("τ′_G ∉ Z_{ρ,σ} par contre on a τ_G ∈ Z_{ρ,σ}"). It also needs i_Z(τ_Z)² = i_{𝒢⁺}(τ²) and conjugation by
  i_Z(τ_Z) to induce int(τ) on 𝒢⁺. Neither is written out.
- **N11 — scan 554 — unpursued implication — keep.** The margin characterises i_𝒢(𝒢) as the inverse image of
  {1, τ_γ}. This needs τ_γ of order 2 and agrees with the statement for ℳ_{ρ,σ} and Γ_{ρ,σ} on scan 563.
- **N12 — scan 554 — unproved claim — keep.** (32bis) follows from (32), since all three coordinates of τ_Z are −1.
- **N13 — scan 555 — unproved claim — keep.** The maps 𝔻_ρ → N_ρ and 𝔻_σ → N_σ are called "évidents". The relations
  needed are τ′ρτ′⁻¹ = ρ⁻¹ and τ′στ′⁻¹ = σ⁻¹ (scan 548, (*)).
- **N14 — scan 555 — unproved claim — correct.** The predicate is legible as "⟺ 𝒢⁺ est isom à un quotient de Z/4Z",
  with no negation. Suppose, as in the modular case, that 𝒢⁺ is topologically generated by ρ and σ with ρ³ = σ² = −1.
  Then ρ = −1 makes 𝒢⁺ = ⟨σ⟩ a quotient of Z/4Z, and conversely a cyclic 𝒢⁺ of order dividing 4 forces ρ² = 1, i.e.
  ρ = −1. So the last term of a chain that begins "i_ρ injectif ⟺ … ρ ≠ −1" should read "n'est pas isom". As written,
  it states the opposite (a slip, or a negation lost in the revision).
- **N15 — scan 556 — unproved claim — keep, corrected.** The corrected text reads "des épimorphismes. On a donc
  diagramme comm. de suites exactes (41)" (row 16), and before (43) "dc diag. comm. de suites exactes" (row 19). The
  page therefore asserts the commutativity of (41) and (43), as well as the surjectivity of δ_ρ and δ_σ.
- **N16 — scan 556 — conjecture (author's doubt) — keep, corrected.** The margin's reservation, "Mais celà ne prouve
  pas que l'on ait commutativité!", faces the main text's "On a donc diagramme comm.". The margin computation shows
  only that every class of γ_{ρ,σ} has a representative a ∈ N_ρ with the right sign. Note C3 shows why the inserted
  star condition (40) settles the right-hand squares.
- **N17 — scan 556 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** The struck conditions a) ρ² ≠ 1 and b) σ² ≠ 1 were replaced by
  the star conditions in (40). The cancelled a) ends "on a commutativité dans" (row 17).
- **N18 — scan 557 — unpursued implication — keep.** The NB says ε_γ refers to the action on L_σ. (43) and (44) do
  read ε_γ (row 18), so γ′ = Ker ε′_γ = Ker ε_{γ,ρ} and γ″ = Ker ε_γ = Ker ε_{γ,σ}. This is consistent with the later
  convention ?′ = Ker ε_{?,ρ}, ?″ = Ker ε_{?,σ} (scan 562).
- **N19 — scan 557 — unproved claim — keep.** (46) identifies SN′_ρ and SN′_σ with the centralisers Cent_{𝒢⁺}(ρ) and
  Cent_{𝒢⁺}(σ) without proof. The annulled display on scan 559 had normalisers instead (N23).
- **N20 — scan 558 — conjecture — keep.** "Une nette tendance à être injectifs (cf (39))" is a qualified expectation.
  (39) gives the criterion ρ² ≠ 1, σ² ≠ 1.
- **N21 — scan 558 — unproved claim — keep.** (47bis) is a margin statement. A stacked mark after "si ρ²≠1 resp.
  σ²≠1" may be "(cf (39))" (unresolved, item 12 of the changes file).
- **N22 — scan 558 — conjecture — keep.** "On verra que dans les 'bons cas'" the restrictions of (49) to Z_ρ and Z_σ
  are epimorphisms. The good cases are not specified.

### Scans 559–562

- **N23 — scan 559 — unproved claim — correct.** The struck margin reads "Annulé — Il paraît que dans le cas que on
  veut considérer, Norm_𝒢(L₀) ≠ Cent_𝒢(L₀) et idem pour L_ρ, L_σ" (row 21). It gives the reason the display was
  annulled: that display identified the kernels with normalisers, while (46) on scan 557 has the centralisers. The
  claim Norm ≠ Cent in the intended cases is not proved.
- **N24 — scan 559 — unproved claim — keep.** The exactness of (52)–(56) is asserted. The kernels follow component by
  component, because every map to γ_{ρ,σ} is an epimorphism.
- **N25 — scan 559 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** One lemma on fibre products of epimorphisms over γ_{ρ,σ} gives
  all four sequences (53)–(56).
- **N26 — scan 560 — unpursued implication — keep.** (57)–(59) are base changes of extensions by L₀. Splitting
  questions are not considered here.
- **N27 — scan 560 — unproved claim — keep, corrected.** The text says that the operation of τ′_ℳ on 𝒢⁺ "est celle
  de τ", yet τ′_ℳ = (τ′_ρ, τ′_σ, τ′_G) is made of avatars of τ′, and (61) maps τ′_𝒢 ↦ τ′_ℳ. Since i_𝒢 is the identity
  on 𝒢⁺, conjugation by τ′_G induces int(τ′), so "τ" looks like a slip for τ′. This should be checked before (61) is
  used.
- **N28 — scan 561 — conjecture — keep.** "À l'exception peut-être des trois flèches issues de γ_{ρ,σ}": the
  surjectivity of χ_γ, ε_{γ,ρ} and ε_{γ,σ} is left open.
- **N29 — scan 561 — unproved claim — keep, corrected.** "Sont cartésiens" is asserted without proof. The count does
  not add up as read: "dix carrés (six faces d'un cube, et [·] carrés marqués I, II, III⁺, IV, V)" gives 6 + 5. The word
  over the struck "quatre" is unread. Each square has to be checked separately.
- **N30 — scan 561 — unpursued implication — keep.** The inclusions of Z_{ρ,σ}, N*_ρ and N*_σ into G_{ρ,σ} are omitted
  from (62) "pour ne pas détruire la commutativité". The defect of commutativity they would introduce is not computed.
- **N31 — scan 561 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Checking only the live vertices of (62) first is sound. The
  inserted "cinq" in c) matches five live arrows marked pr; the sixth, dashed pr arrow belongs to the blackened node.
- **N32 — scan 562 — unproved claim — keep.** The cube (65) is called "cartésien" without proof. The inverse images of
  the coordinate subgroups under (χ_?, ε_{?,ρ}, ε_{?,σ}) give it directly (N34).
- **N33 — scan 562 — unproved claim — keep.** f) "Rappelons aussi qu'on a commutativité dans (66)": a recall of
  (32bis), which needs the map {±1} → Ẑ* (−1 ↦ −1).
- **N34 — scan 562 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** The struck margin, now read "… N′_ρ, N′_σ déjà utilisée dans un
  autre sens" (row 27), is the author's own remark that the general notation ?′ conflicts with the N′ of scan 557.

### Scans 563–567

- **N35 — scan 563 — unproved claim — keep.** "En prenant les noyaux … on trouve un diagramme de m̄ type
  combinatoire": the identification of the kernels is not written out on the page. N36 lists the kernels.
- **N36 — scan 563 — own idea (first pass) — keep, corrected.** The nine groups are S₀ℳ, ℳ, S₀Γ, Γ, Z_{ρ,σ}, N*_ρ,
  N*_σ, G_{ρ,σ} and γ_{ρ,σ}. Their kernels in γ_{ρ,σ} are L₀×SN*_ρ×SN*_σ×𝒢⁺, SN*_ρ×SN*_σ×𝒢⁺, L₀×SN*_ρ×SN*_σ,
  SN*_ρ×SN*_σ, L₀, SN*_ρ, SN*_σ, 𝒢⁺ and {1}: nine groups, as scan 564 says. This supports the reading "9 groupes".
  𝒢 and 𝒢⁺ are inserted "comme … de deux flèches pointillées" (row 28), not in a parenthesis.
- **N37 — scan 564 — unpursued implication — keep.** The empty product {1} = Ker(γ →id γ) completes the pattern of
  N36. The passage also says that SN*_ρ, SN*_σ and 𝒢⁺ are not drawn "également" (row 29), not "séparément".
- **N38 — scan 565 — unproved claim — correct.** The σ-clause of the NB reads "ε′ est déterminé par ξ,η" (row 33),
  where the sense requires ε. If σ is not 𝒢⁺-conjugate to σ⁻¹, then ησ determines whether it is conjugate to σ or to
  σ⁻¹, and hence ε. μ is determined by ξ and η only when n ↦ ε₀^n is injective.
- **N39 — scan 565 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** (69) η⁻¹ξ ∈ L₁ drops the condition μ ∈ Ẑ*. It is equivalent to
  ξ = ηε₁^{μ−1} only when every element of L₁ has the form ε₁^{μ−1} with μ a unit.
- **N40 — scan 565 — unproved claim — keep, with proof.** "Évidemment surjective" holds. Given
  (ξ,η,μ,ε′,ε) ∈ Σ_{ρ,σ}, write ξρ = gρ^{ε′}g⁻¹ with g ∈ 𝒢⁺ and put α = gτ′^{ν′}, where (−1)^{ν′} = ε′. Since
  τ′ρτ′⁻¹ = ρ⁻¹, αρα⁻¹ = ξρ, so [α,ρ] = ξ and ε_𝒢(α) = ε′. β is obtained in the same way, and the relation of (70) is
  then ξ = ηε₁^{μ−1}.
- **N41 — scan 565 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** The fibres of (71) are cosets of Cent_𝒢(ρ)×Cent_𝒢(σ) acting by
  right multiplication on (α,β), with the sign conditions fixing the cosets of 𝒢⁺.
- **N42 — scan 566 — unproved claim — keep.** "Cet u sera alors unique" (if ρ and σ generate 𝒢⁺ topologically and u is
  continuous). The inserted converse is "et inversement, la conn. de u détermine ξ,η".
- **N43 — scan 566 — unproved claim — keep.** The bijection (73) Z_{ρ,σ} ≅ Σ^eff_{ρ,σ} relies on "or cet ensemble
  n'est autre que le sous-groupe Z_{ρ,σ} … (cf (6) p. 577′)", which refers to scan 549.
- **N44 — scan 566 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Membership in Σ^eff requires an automorphism u with prescribed
  values on ρ and σ. No intrinsic criterion for it is given on these pages.
- **N45 — scan 567 — unproved claim — keep.** (76) needs the embeddings i_ρ and i_σ, as the margin says ("pour être
  plus correct, il faudrait écrire α = u i_ρ(a)⁻¹ τ′^{ν′(x)}, …").
- **N46 — scan 567 — unproved claim (proof begun) — keep.** "Je dis que l'application envisagée (76) est injective":
  (78) recovers a and b from u, α, β and μ. Recovering u itself from (α,β,μ) is left at the page break.
- **N47 — scan 567 — own idea (first pass) — keep.** Whether (76) is onto SG^eff_{ρ,σ} needs the conditions
  a ∈ N*_ρ and b ∈ N*_σ and the equality of the images in γ_{ρ,σ}. These are not checked on the page.

## New notes (Claude, second pass)

- **C1 — scans 549, 559 — own idea (textual history), Claude (second pass).** The group Z_{ρ,σ} was first written
  B_{ρ,σ}: Z is written over B in (6) and in (50) (rows 2 and 23), and the struck display on scan 559 still has
  SB_{ρ,σ} = Ker(B_{ρ,σ} →δ_B γ_{ρ,σ}) ≃ L₀. The name recalls the group of §48 II ("l'équation ζ = η … et le groupe
  B₀ B_{ρ,σ}", Circle table p. 434). Comparing the two definitions would show what the renaming changed. Confidence:
  medium.
- **C2 — scan 554 — conjecture (question), Claude (second pass).** Which of τ and τ′ satisfies u(ε₀) = ε₀^{−1} for
  u = int(·)|𝒢⁺, as the coordinate χ = −1 of τ_Z in (32) requires? The page switches from τ′ to τ = στ′ (row 11), and
  the margin asserts τ′_G ∉ Z_{ρ,σ} and τ_G ∈ Z_{ρ,σ}. Before (31)–(32) are used, this should be checked against the
  matrices that §44 fixes for τ, τ′, σ and ε₀. Confidence: low–medium; this is only a check.
- **C3 — scan 556 — own idea, Claude (second pass).** The right-hand squares of (41) and (43) commute by the definition
  (40) of the starred groups. For x ∈ N*_ρ, ε′_γ(δ_ρ(x)) = ε′_γ(δ_G(i_ρ(x))) = ε′_G(i_ρ(x)) = ε_ρ(x) by (28), and in
  the same way ε_γ∘δ_σ = ε_σ on N*_σ. The left-hand arrows Z_ρ → γ′ and Z_σ → γ″ also need Z_ρ ⊂ N*_ρ, i.e. ε′_G = 1
  on Z_ρ, which holds when ρ is not 𝒢⁺-conjugate to ρ⁻¹. The margin's reservation therefore applies to the unstarred
  N_ρ, and the inserted star condition is what removes it. Confidence: high for the right-hand squares, medium for the
  left.
- **C4 — scan 564 — own idea (textual), Claude (second pass).** "Nous avons inséré dans (62) seulement ~~cinq~~ parmi
  ces groupes produits": the number is struck and not replaced. Besides the blackened node, (62) as drawn shows four
  products of L₀, SN*_ρ, SN*_σ and 𝒢⁺: L₀×SN*_ρ×SN*_σ, L₀×SN*_ρ×SN*_σ×𝒢⁺, SN*_ρ×SN*_σ and SN*_ρ×SN*_σ×𝒢⁺. This may be
  why the count was dropped. Confidence: low.


# ===== LM_C3-568-586 (checked notes) =====

# LM_C3-568-586: research notes checked against the corrected transcription (scans 568–586)

**Source:** the first pass's research notes in `answers/unzipped/LM_C3-568-586/LM_C3-568-586_notes.md` (§ "Research
notes"). Each note was checked against `LM_C3-568-586_checked.tex`. "Row n" refers to `LM_C3-568-586_changes.md`.

**Verdicts:**
- keep: the note stands as written.
- correct: the note is revised; the reason is given.
- drop: the note is withdrawn.

**Kinds:**
- conjecture: Grothendieck's conjectures, questions, expectations and programme.
- unproved claim: a claim without proof in the cited passage.
- unpursued implication.
- own idea.

Notes marked **Claude (second pass)** are new.

These pages are §49 V ("Les groupes M_{ρ,σ} généraux (contexte profini)", Circle table pp. 577–627), upper numbers
588–596. They contain the end of 6° (the bijection S₀Γ_{ρ,σ} ≅ S𝔊^{eff}_{ρ,σ}), "7° Fonctorialités" and
"8° Le cas universel 𝒢 = Gl(2,Z)^∧".

## Notes of the first pass

### Scans 568–572

- **N1 — scan 568 — unproved claim — correct.**
  - Freeness of the action (81) is immediate: (a₀,b₀) acts by right translation α ↦ αa₀, β ↦ βb₀.
  - The action preserves 𝔊_{ρ,σ} because [αa₀,ρ] = [α,ρ] exactly when a₀ commutes with ρ. That is why the inserted
    "(= SZ_ρ × SZ_σ)" stands beside the acting group.
  - The quotient is identified with Σ_{ρ,σ}, not Σ̄ (row 2). The real content is that the orbits are the whole fibres
    of (α,β,μ) ↦ ([α,ρ],[β,σ],μ), with the S- and *-variants.
- **N2 — scans 568–569 — unpursued implication — keep, corrected.** In (83) the a-coordinate is twisted by τ^{ν′} and
  the b-coordinate by τ^ν, with ε = (−1)^ν and ε′ = (−1)^{ν′} fixed by (82). (83) then becomes left translation by
  (τ^{ν′},τ^ν)(a₀,b₀)⁻¹. The cancelled bottom of 568 had the same shape, with ε^{τ′}_ρ(a₀⁻¹), ε^{τ′}_σ(b₀⁻¹) and the
  acting group written SZ′_ρ × SZ′_σ (row 3).
- **N3 — scan 569 — unproved claim — correct.** The "clair à priori" can be verified in two lines.
  - For (α,β,μ) ↦ (ℓα,ℓβ,μ) to preserve [α,ρ] = [β,σ]ε₁^{μ−1}, one needs
    ε₁^{μ−1}·int(ρ)(ℓ⁻¹) = int(σ)(ℓ⁻¹)·ε₁^{μ−1} for ℓ ∈ L₀ = ε₀^Ẑ.
  - Since ε₀ = σρ (scan 574, (11)) and ρ³ = σ², we have int(ρ)(ε₀) = int(σ)(ε₀) = ρσ.
  - For the matrices of scan 572, ρσ = (1 0; 1 1) = ε₁. Both sides are then powers of ε₁, which commute.
- **N4 — scan 569, (87) — unproved claim — keep, corrected.** The exponents read ν′_ρ(a) on α and ν_σ(b) on β. The
  second pass agrees, and they match (83), where a carries ν′ and b carries ν. Bijectivity follows from (84) as
  stated: (84) recovers (u,a,b) from (α,β,μ), and f = uU⁻¹ then gives U.
- **N5 — scan 570, proposition (89)–(92) — unproved claim — keep.** The NB after c) is longer than the first pass had
  it: "φ_Z sera unique par/pour [glyph], et est compatible avec i_{L₀,Z}" (row 9).
- **N6 — scan 570 — own idea (first pass) — keep.**
- **N7 — scan 571, corollary (93) — unproved claim — correct.**
  - The ink names the source and target S₀G^𝒢_{ρ,σ} and S₀G^{𝒢′}_{ρ,σ}, with G as in (92) (row 10), while the
    map is called φ_M.
  - (94) and the first line of 572 speak of S₀M. Either G is a slip for M, or φ_M is first defined on S₀G.
  - The earlier definitions of S₀G and S₀M, before scan 568, should settle it.
- **N8 — scan 571, "Exemple" — unproved claim — keep.**
- **N9 — scans 571–572, "Rmq" and (94) — unpursued implication — correct.**
  - (94) defines G^♮_{ρ,σ} = Z^♮_{ρ,σ}·𝒢⁺ with "=" (row 12).
  - The margin requires τ′_Z ∈ Z^*_{ρ,σ} with an asterisk (row 11), and scan 572 then writes S₀M^*_{ρ,σ}, so both
    signs are used for the restricted objects.
  - The condition puts τ′ in G^♮, so the sign character ε_G is still defined on it.
- **N10 — scans 571–572 — own idea (first pass) — drop.** "Test the variant on subgroups generated by the required
  involution and a few automorphisms" names no concrete statement to test. N9 gives the concrete role of τ′_Z.
- **Closing remark (no conjecture in 568–572) — keep.**

### Scans 573–577

- **573-R1 — scan 573, (2) — unproved claim — keep, with a proof route.**
  - 𝒢̂⁺/{±1} = PSL(2,Z)^∧ is the free profinite product Z/2 ⨿ Z/3, which has trivial centre. Hence
    Centr(𝒢̂⁺) = {±1}.
  - For M = Ñ_{1,1}: by (5), M/{±1} = M̃_{0,3} acts faithfully on the centre-free group 𝒢̂⁺_{0,3}. If M̃_{0,3}
    contains the inner automorphisms, its centre is trivial: an automorphism that commutes with every int(x) of a
    centre-free group is the identity.
- **573-R2 — scan 573, (3)–(5) — unproved claim — keep.**
- **573-R3 — scans 573–574 — unproved claim — keep, completed.**
  - The kernel elements are x ↦ x·χ(x) with χ ∈ Hom(𝒢̂⁺,{±1}) and χ(−1) = 1; bijectivity needs the second
    condition.
  - Hom(𝒢̂⁺,{±1}) = Hom(Z/12, Z/2) has order 2, and sg(−1) = 1 because −1 ∈ Π̂. So the kernel is {1, Θ}, as in
    (6)–(9).
- **574-R1 — scan 574, (7)–(9) — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **574-R2 — scans 574–575 — unproved claim — correct.** One line settles the nonconjugacy of ε₀ and (−ε₀)^μ = −ε₀^μ:
  map 𝒢 to GL(2,Ẑ). There tr(ε₀) = 2 and tr(−ε₀^μ) = −2 for every μ ∈ Ẑ^*, and 2 ≠ −2 in Ẑ. Grothendieck's route goes
  through the abelianised quotient and l₀ = ε₀² and is longer. The group in "même dans Sl(2,Ẑ)′|!" is uncertain
  (row 19).
- **575-R1 — scan 575, (12) — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **575-R2 — scan 575, conditions a), b) — own idea (first pass) — correct (answered).** Π̂ is characteristic in 𝒢̂⁺,
  so condition a) holds for every automorphism.
  - Every automorphism preserves the centre {±1} (stated on 573).
  - Π̂/{±1} is the kernel of a surjection of PSL(2,Z)^∧ = Z/2 ⨿ Z/3 onto S₃. Such a surjection must send the order-2
    generator to a transposition and the order-3 generator to a 3-cycle.
  - Aut(S₃) = S₃ permutes the six (transposition, 3-cycle) pairs simply transitively. So all these surjections have
    the same kernel.
  - Only b) is a real condition. The image of (3) is the set of u with u(ε₀) conjugate to some ε₀^μ, and the
    check "u stabilise Π̂" on scan 581 is automatic.
  - Confidence high: an elementary argument.
- **576-R1 — scan 576, (14) — unproved claim — correct.**
  - The reference reads "cf. \overline{\underline{III}}, pp. 665…" (row 20).
  - The Circle table puts §49 III, "Passages de M̃_{0,3} à Ñ_{1,1}", which contains the cited construction, at
    p. 555. The clearly written 665 does not match; this is unresolved.
- **576-R2 — scan 576, converse — unproved claim — correct.** The page is 390 (row 21), the start of §46 I "Les groupes
  M̃_{0,3} et Ñ^!_{0,3}". "Par définition" refers to the definition of M̃_{0,3} there, so the reference is resolved.
- **576-R3 — scan 576, 1° and 2° — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **577-R1 — scan 577, (16)–(18) — unproved claim — correct.** The verification is immediate from (17).
  - Π_{0,3} is free on l₀, l₁, with l_∞ = (l₁l₀)⁻¹.
  - (−l_∞)(−l₁)(−l₀) = −l_∞l₁l₀ = 1, so l_i ↦ λ_i is a homomorphism.
  - It is a section because λ_i ≡ l_i mod ±1. The profinite case follows by completion.
- **577-R2 — scan 577, proposition and (19) — unproved claim — correct.** (19) reads σ, not σ′, with σ(λ₀) = λ₁ and
  σ(λ₁) = λ₀ (rows 22–23). The clean margin checks this through σ(λ_∞) = λ₁⁻¹λ₀⁻¹ and τ(λ_∞) = λ₀λ₁. Normality under M
  is done on 578 through M = M(0)·𝒢^{+∧}.
- **577-R3 — scan 577 — own idea (first pass) — correct (answered).** Π₀ is the only complement of μ in Π that 𝒢
  normalises, and likewise Π̂₀ in Π̂.
  - Complements correspond to Hom(Π₀,μ) ≅ F₂², since Π₀ is free of rank 2.
  - Since Π₀ is normal in 𝒢, 𝒢 acts linearly on this space. On Π₀^{ab}/2 = F₂² the element ρ cycles λ₀, λ₁, λ_∞ and
    σ swaps λ₀, λ₁, so 𝒢 acts through S₃ = GL(2,F₂), transitively on the nonzero vectors. No other complement is
    invariant.
  - Confidence high.
- **Closing remark (no conjecture in 573–577) — keep.**

### Scans 578–582

- **578-a — scan 578, reduction to M(0) — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **578-b — scan 578, (22) — unproved claim — correct.** The one-line proof given is complete. A section of the central
  extension (15) with image Π₀ gives Π = Π₀ × μ: μ is central, Π₀ ∩ μ = 1, and Π₀·μ = Π. The profinite case is the
  same.
- **578-c — scan 578, (20)–(21) — own idea (first pass) — drop.** The computation uses only (−1)^μ = −1 for
  μ ∈ Ẑ^* and the shape of u(ρ) in (20). No hypothesis to generalise is identified.
- **579-a — scan 579, (27) — unproved claim — keep, completed for Gl(2,Z).** In Gl(2,Z), Z(ρ) = ⟨ρ⟩ and Z(σ) = ⟨σ⟩,
  and they meet Π·{1,τ} and Π in {±1}. So α and β are determined exactly up to sign. In the completion the same
  centraliser statements are still needed.
- **579-b — scan 579, (28)–(29) — unproved claim — correct.**
  - With the second line of (28) live (row 26), (28) is a presentation of Gl(2,Z) in ρ, σ, τ plus τ(ρ) = ρ:
    ρ⁶ = σ⁴ = τ² = 1, ρ³ = σ², τ(ρ) = σ(ρ⁻¹), τ(σ) = σ⁻¹.
  - By (29) the added relation is λ₁ = 1, and its ρ-conjugates kill λ₀ and λ_∞, so it generates Π₀ normally.
  - Still to cite: that the first two lines present Gl(2,Z) (Sl(2,Z) = Z/4 ∗_{Z/2} Z/6). The orders agree: (31) is
    the dicyclic group of order 12 = [Sl(2,Z):Π₀], and (28) has order 24.
- **579-c — scan 579, (23)–(26) — own idea (first pass) — correct.** The doubtful generator decides the matter.
  - With μ_σ = {1,σ} as the ink looks, σ ∈ 𝒢⁺ would give D_σ ∩ 𝒢⁺ = D_σ ⊋ μ, against (24).
  - An involution of determinant −1 such as τ′ = (0 1; 1 0) satisfies all of (24). The transcription keeps the
    doubt σ|τ′.
- **580-a — scan 580, "Quelle est l'opération" — conjecture/question — keep.**
- **580-b — scan 580, (32)–(34) — unproved claim — correct.** There is no gap: this is the standard correspondence.
  Automorphisms of a central extension that are the identity on kernel and quotient correspond to Hom(quotient,
  kernel), by θ ↦ (x ↦ θ(x)x⁻¹). Hom(S₃, Z/2) = Z/2, generated by Θ(x) = sg(x)·x.
- **580-c — scan 580 — own idea (first pass) — keep.**
- **581-a — scan 581, left margin — unproved claim — correct.** The margin reads [u̇,ẋ] = sg(x)^{ν₂(u)} in
  𝒢^{+∧}/Π₀, for u ∈ M^! and x ∈ 𝒢^{+∧} (row 31). The exponent is retraced. The warning's wording is unresolved.
- **581-b — scan 581, (36)–(37) — unproved claim — correct.** Both prerequisites are elementary.
  - The abelianisation Z/12 of (6) sends ρ ↦ 2, ρ⁻¹ ↦ 10, σ ↦ 3 and σ⁻¹ ↦ 9. So ρ and ρ⁻¹, and σ and σ⁻¹, are not
    𝒢^{+∧}-conjugate.
  - n ↦ ε₀^n is injective on Ẑ because ε₀ has order N in Sl(2,Z/N) for every N.
- **581-c — scan 581, lower proof — unpursued implication — keep.** By 575-R2 the check "u stabilise Π̂" is automatic;
  the substance is the third line of (36), u(L₀) = L₀.
- **581-d — scan 581, (35) — own idea (first pass) — correct.**
  - The main text has Θ^{ε₂(u)} (row 29); the margin has the exponent ν₂(u).
  - Θ² = 1, so Θ^{±1} = Θ, and (35) as written would give Θ for every u.
  - The arrows σ̇ ↦ ε₂(u)σ̇ need the exponent ν₂(u) ∈ Z/2, with ε₂ = (−1)^{ν₂}. The main formula has a notational
    slip that the margin avoids.
- **582-a — scan 582, margin — unproved claim (correction) — keep.** "Cf rectification plus détaillée à p. 609 ff."
  points to a later page of §49 V. The margin's last line, Z^!_{ρ,σ} = M^![0] = Z_{ρ,σ} ∩ M^!, is the form in which
  the Proposition is stated.
- **582-b — scan 582, (38)–(40) — unproved claim — keep.**
- **582-c — scan 582, corollary — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **582-d — scan 582 — own idea (first pass, editorial) — drop.** It is editorial, not mathematical. The corrected
  transcription already keeps the margin, the main line and the reference apart.

### Scans 583–586

- **583-a — scan 583, after (42) — conjecture (expectation, rejected on these pages) — keep.**
- **583-b — scan 583, (43)–(43 ter) — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **584-a — scan 584, (45) — unpursued implication — keep.** β″ = u′(β)β′ is a crossed-homomorphism identity, and b″ = b′b
  follows from it, as the page shows.
- **584-b — scan 584, (46) — unproved claim — correct.**
  - (46) reads "u(ρ) 𝒢^{+∧}-conj. à ρ^{ε₃}", with "est" struck (row 35).
  - The needed distinctness ρ ≁ ρ⁻¹ comes from the Z/12 abelianisation (581-b).
  - Then u′u(ρ) ~ u′(ρ^{ε₃}) ~ ρ^{ε′₃ε₃}, which gives ε″₃ = ε′₃ε₃.
- **584-c — scan 584, a″ = a′a and (*) — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **585-a — scan 585, (47)–(48) — unpursued implication — keep.**
- **585-b — scan 585, "condition draconienne" — unproved claim — correct.** For Γ_Q the claim is a classical fact.
  - If g commutes with complex conjugation c, g maps the real algebraic numbers R_alg = Fix(c) to themselves.
  - R_alg has no automorphism but the identity: the ordering is given by squares, and each element is fixed by its
    cut in Q.
  - So the centraliser of c is {1,c}, and "u′ ∈ {1,τ_γ}" holds for Γ_Q. The open part is the analogue for Γ̃_Q.
- **585-c — scan 585, "arguments heuristiques" — conjecture — keep.** The expected centraliser of τ_γ in Γ̃_Q is
  {1,τ_γ}. It is the Γ̃_Q (Grothendieck–Teichmüller-type) analogue of 585-b; the literature is still to check.
- **585-d — scan 585, final paragraph and cancelled margin — unpursued implication — correct.** The cancelled margin
  reads "γ^!_{ρ,σ} = Ker(γ_{ρ,σ} → …)", with the "!" of M^! (row 36), not γ′. The refinement it begins is taken up
  in the last line, "se borner à des u ∈ M^![0]′" (see C2).
- **585-e — scan 585, (47) — own idea (first pass) — keep.**
- **586-a — scan 586, opening — conjecture/construction goal — keep.**
- **586-b — scan 586, after (49 bis) — unproved claim — keep, completed for Gl(2,Z).** In Gl(2,Z) the centralisers are
  Z(σ) = ⟨σ⟩ (order 4) and Z(ρ) = ⟨ρ⟩ (order 6). No element of determinant −1 commutes with σ or ρ: the commutant
  matrices aI + bσ and aI + bρ have determinants a² + b² and a² + ab + b², which are never −1. This is the source of
  i ∈ Z/4Z and j ∈ Z/6Z. In 𝒢^{+∧} the same is needed.
- **586-c — scan 586, Proposition (50) — unpursued implication — keep.** (i,j) ↦ (a,b) is injective, because a and b
  determine f′ and f. So each fibre has 24 elements once the centralisers in 𝒢^{+∧} are ⟨ρ⟩ and ⟨σ⟩. The insertion in
  (50) reads "avec" (row 37).
- **586-d — scan 586, final paragraph — own idea (first pass) — keep, corrected.** "i.e. ε₃(τ′) = σ^{ν₃}" (row 38)
  contradicts the definition just above, where ε₃(τ′) = τ′ if ε₃ = −1. τ′^{ν₃} is meant. The choice "i = 0|j, j = 0"
  is unresolved.

## New notes (Claude, second pass)

- **C1 — scan 568, kernel of (u,a,b) ↦ u — unproved claim.** "de noyau SN^*_ρ × SN^*_σ = SZ_ρ × SZ_σ" asserts that the
  starred S-normalisers equal the S-centralisers. This is the same equality that makes the acting group of (81)
  "(= SZ_ρ × SZ_σ)". It is used here without proof and should come from the definitions of N^* before scan 568.
- **C2 — scans 584–585, restriction to M^![0]′ — own idea.** Confidence medium.
  - By (48), the δ-image (*) of a″ = a′a holds whenever ν₃(u) = 0 for the right-hand factor u, because the condition
    [δ(a′), τ_γ^{ν₃}] = 1 is then empty.
  - So (*) holds on Ker(ε₃), and M^![0]′ = M^![0] ∩ Ker(ε₃) fits the cancelled margin's Ker(γ^!_{ρ,σ} —ε₃→ …).
  - The page does not define M^![0]′, and (*) is only the δ-image of multiplicativity.
- **C3 — scan 575, Proposition — unpursued implication.** Combined with 575-R2, the Proposition says that M̃_{0,3} is
  isomorphic to the group of automorphisms u of 𝒢̂⁺ with u(ε₀) conjugate to ε₀^μ for some μ ∈ Ẑ^*. This is a
  characterisation by one peripheral class, as in (36) on scan 581.
- **C4 — scan 576 — unpursued implication.**
  - The two references locate the inputs: the definition of M̃_{0,3} (p. 390, §46 I) and the construction of Ñ_{1,1}
    from M̃_{0,3}.
  - That construction should be §49 III at p. 555, while the ink reads 665.
  - The normalisation u₀(ε₀) = ε₀^μ in (14) is used in (20), (36) and (38). Pinning down the 665|555 reference would
    make the necessity half of the Proposition self-contained.


# ===== LM_C3-587-605 (checked notes) =====

# LM_C3-587-605 — research notes, checked against the corrected transcription (scans 587–605)

Source: the first pass's research notes (answers/unzipped/LM_C3-587-605/LM_C3-587-605_notes.md, "Research notes —
English", 67 notes, cited here as FP scan-n), each checked against LM_C3-587-605_checked.tex. Verdicts: keep, correct
(text revised), drop. Notes marked **Claude (second pass)** are new. Kinds: conjecture (Grothendieck's conjectures,
questions, expectations), unproved claim, unpursued implication, own idea. "Unproved" means not proved in the cited
passage.

Setting (§49.V, "Les groupes Mρ,σ généraux (contexte profini)"): Z!_{ρ,σ} = M![0]; S₀Γ!_{ρ,σ} is an extension of Z! by
L_ρ × L_σ ≃ ℤ/6 × ℤ/4 with elements (u, a, b); A is the set-theoretic section u ↦ (u, a = ε₃(τ′)α⁻¹u, b = β⁻¹u) with α
normalised in Π̂_{0τ} = {1, τ}·Π̂₀ (51). Conventions used in the checks, taken from the pages: x(y) = xyx⁻¹ and
[x, y] = xyx⁻¹y⁻¹ (592 expands [u̇′, σ̇] as u̇′(σ̇)σ̇⁻¹); τ′ is an involution (ε₃″(τ′) = ε₃′(τ′)ε₃(τ′), 589);
σ² = ρ³ = −1, central modulo Π̂₀ (presentation on 605). In F₂-coordinates e₃, e₂ ∈ {0, 1}, e = 1 means sign −1.

## Scan 587

- **FP 587-1 — conjecture (question) — correct.** "je me demande si cette section ensembliste … est multiplicative".
  The pages answer it: A is multiplicative over Z^{!′} (ε₃ = 1) and Z^{!‴} (ε₂ = ε₃) but "n'est pas multiplicative au
  dessus de M![0] tout entier" (590); the defect is the sign ε of (60) and (63).
- **FP 587-2 — unproved claim, cancelled — keep.** The struck "Corollaire La section envisagée est multiplicative" is
  exactly what 588–590 refute; the cancellation is justified by them.
- **FP 587-3 — own idea — drop.** Computing the multiplicative defect is what Grothendieck does: (53) on 588, a″ = εa′a
  with ε from (63), and α″ in (65).
- **Claude (second pass) — 587, Proposition (52) — unproved claim.** Sufficiency reduces (52) to σ̇ⁱ = ρ̇ʲ = 1 and then
  concludes i = j = 0; this needs i and j to be read modulo the orders of σ̇ and ρ̇ in the quotient by Π₀ (4 and 6 by
  the presentation on 605). Their ranges are fixed in (50), before this package, and are not restated here.

## Scan 588

- **FP 588-1 — conjecture (expectation), cancelled — keep.** The proposal to replace fibre products over
  G_{ρ,σ}/G⁺_{ρ,σ} by fibre products over the finer G_{ρ,σ}/Π₀ is struck with its paragraph and is not taken up here.
- **FP 588-2 — unproved claim, cancelled — correct.** "un rev. de degré 12": the group of 605,
  Sl(2,ℤ)/Π₀ = ⟨ρ, σ | ρ⁶ = σ⁴ = 1, ρ³ = σ², σρσ⁻¹ = ρ⁻¹⟩, is dicyclic of order 12, which fits a degree-12 covering;
  the abbreviation before "universel" is still unread (rev.|gr.).
- **FP 588-3 — unpursued implication — correct.** The criterion is not symmetric in the pair: A(u′u) = A(u′)A(u) iff
  ε₃(u) = 1 or ε₂(u′)ε₃(u′) = 1 (59 bis). If ε₃(u) = 1, ε₂(u) = −1 and ε₃(u′) = −1, the product u′u is multiplicative
  while uu′ is not. Revised from "check the reversed order".
- **FP 588-4 — own idea — correct (checked).** The cancelled table 54bis agrees with (55) on 589 (ε₃ = 1: always; ε₃ =
  −1: σ(a′) ≡ ε₃′a′) and with (56) (σ(u′) ≡ ±u′). It was struck together with the first (54), which 589 restates.
- **Claude (second pass) — 588, (53) and the NB — unpursued implication.** Cancelling ε₃′(σ) gives ε₃(σ)⁻¹(a′), not
  ε₃(σ)(a′); the NB "ε₃(σ) et ε₃(σ⁻¹) opèrent de la m̄ façon" licenses the change because σ² = −1 is central modulo
  Π̂₀. The blotted exponent at σ in (53) fits this; the checked text reads σ|σ⁻¹.

## Scan 589

- **FP 589-1 — unproved claim — keep.** (35), u̇′(σ̇) = ε₂(u′)σ̇ modulo Π̂₀, is quoted, not proved; (35 bis) is used
  the same way on 598 and 605.
- **FP 589-2 — unpursued implication — correct.** Answered at once: the Proposition (589–590, box d)) makes a″ = a′a
  equivalent to "ε₃ = 1 ou ε₂′ = ε₃′"; only ε₃ = −1 needs (58).
- **FP 589-3 — own idea — correct.** Carried out by Grothendieck: Z^{!‴} = {ε₂(u) = ε₃(u)} in (59), and the kernel of
  (ε₂, ε₃) in (81).

## Scan 590

- **FP 590-1 — unpursued implication — keep.** The gloss "i.e. a(ρ) = ρ" turns ε₃ = 1 into a stabiliser condition;
  whether it depends on the chosen ρ, σ is not discussed.
- **FP 590-2 — unproved claim — correct.** "elle n'est pas multiplicative au dessus de M![0] tout entier" needs u with
  ε₃(u) = −1 and u′ with ε₂(u′)ε₃(u′) = −1. 604 notes that all elements coming from Gl(2,ℤ) have ε₂ε₃ = 1 and turns to
  u = diag(μ, 1), μ ≡ 5 (12), in Gl(2,ℤ̂); the existence of such u in M![0] is the missing input.
- **FP 590-3 — own idea — correct.** Done on 599–600: S₀Γ⁰ = S₀Γ^{!′} ∩ S₀Γ^{!‴} (81), and S₀^{Π₀}Γ^{!0}, of
  index 8 in S₀^ΠΓ!, is shown normal.

## Scan 591

- **FP 591-1 — conjecture (question) — correct.** "Je n'ai pas trouvé de jolie expression analogue" concerns the group
  "caractérisé par la relation ε₂(u)ε₃(u)=1", i.e. S₀Γ^{!‴} of (59); the ink writes S₀Γ^{!″} (two primes), a slip (in
  (81) Γ^{!″} names a third subgroup). The search for an expression is left open.

  (57), L_ρ ∩ Π̂ = L_σ ∩ Π̂ = μ and L_ρ ∩ Π̂₀ = L_σ ∩ Π̂₀ = {1}, with N*_ρ ∩ 𝒢̃ = L_ρ and N*_σ ∩ 𝒢̃ = L_σ.
- **FP 591-4 — own idea — drop.** Comparing the moduli Π̂₀ and Π̂ is the content of 592, where (56) is read "dans M/Π̂
  (au lieu de M/Π̂₀)".

## Scan 592

- **FP 592-1 — unpursued implication — correct.** Answered on 593 and 600: modulo Π̂ the defect disappears (S₀^ΠΓ! is a
  group), and what survives is the extension of Z! by μ, reduced on 600 to the finite 𝓔.
- **FP 592-2 — unproved claim — keep.** (57) is asserted without derivation; it determines the kernel μ.

## Scan 593

- **FP 593-1 — unproved claim — correct.** Corrected reading: "Les extensions par L₋ρ × L_σ … munies deux sections
  canoniques" (not "munie d'une section"): the extensions by L₋ρ and by L_σ each carry a canonical section, leaving the
  extension by μ. The sections are not constructed on this page, and the splitting of L_ρ × L_σ into μ × L₋ρ × L_σ
  compatibly with the action of Z! is not discussed.
- **FP 593-2 — unpursued implication — keep.** The residual extension is read as the sign ambiguity of α above
  α̃ ∈ Π̂/±1; the normalisation α ∈ Π̂_{0τ} picks a representative but, as 594 says, not a multiplicative one.
- **FP 593-3 — own idea — correct.** The cocycle is computed by Grothendieck on 594–595: A(u′)A(u) and A(u′u) differ by
  ε, with ε = −1 iff ε₃(u) = −1 and ε₂(u′)ε₃(u′) = −1 (63). See the Claude note under 602.

## Scan 594

- **FP 594-1 — unpursued implication — keep.** "pour canonique qu'il soit, n'est pas multiplicatif": a preferred
  set-theoretic section, not a splitting.
- **FP 594-2 — own idea — correct (settled).** The ink of "i.e. si ε₃(u)=−1 et ε₂(u′)ε₃(u′)=+1" is clear (crop); it is a
  slip, since the case contrary to (59 bis) needs −1, which (63) on 595 writes. The diplomatic text keeps +1.
- **FP 594-3 — unproved claim — correct.** (60) follows from the Proposition of 591: A(u″) and the product
  A(u′)A(u) lie in the subgroup S₀^ΠΓ! over the same u″, so they differ by an element (1, ε, 1) of the kernel μ.

## Scan 595

- **FP 595-1 — conjecture — keep.** "Je présume qu'on aura (64)" (no "et" in the ink) with the margin "pas vrai en
  général": a tentative formula, rejected on the same page.
- **FP 595-2 — unpursued implication — keep.** For ε₃ = −1, (64) holds iff a′ commutes with τ′ ("restriction
  draconienne"); the shorter formula is valid exactly on that centraliser.
- **FP 595-3 — own idea — correct (verified).** From (*) α″ = εu′ua⁻¹a′⁻¹st and u′(α)α′ = u′ua⁻¹sa′⁻¹t, with
  s = ε₃(τ′), t = ε₃′(τ′), s² = t² = 1, one gets (εu′(α)α′)⁻¹α″ = t[a′, s]t⁻¹ = [t(a′), s]; this is the page's final
  formula ε₃′(τ′)([a′, ε₃(τ′)]) = [ε₃′(τ′)(a′), ε₃(τ′)].
- **FP 595-4 — own idea — drop.** Merged into FP 594-2 (the sign is settled).

## Scan 596

- **FP 596-1 — conjecture (question) — correct.** Partly answered: 600 reduces the extension S₀^ΠΓ! of Z! by μ to the
  pull-back of the finite central extension 𝓔 of μ × μ by μ, and 603 shows 𝓔 is not split. See the Claude note below
  for what this leaves open.

  over Z^{!′} and Z^{!‴}) and the Rmque on 591 (its values satisfy u ≡ b ≡ ε₃(σ)a mod Π̂₀).
- **FP 596-3 — unpursued implication — keep.** (65) rewrites the correction term through α′⁻¹u′.
- **FP 596-4 — own idea — drop.** The intersection and its normality are worked out on 599–600 ((81), (82),
  Proposition b)).
- **Claude (second pass) — 596, (65) — unpursued implication.** Substituting the product ε₃′(τ′)a′ = α′⁻¹u′ for the
  conjugate ε₃′(τ′)(a′) of 595 is legitimate: for commuting t, s ∈ {1, τ′}, [ta′t⁻¹, s] = [ta′, s].
- **Claude (second pass) — 596, the main question — unpursued implication; confidence medium.** 𝓔 non-split does not
  by itself make S₀^ΠΓ! non-split: its class is the inflation of that of 𝓔 along Z! → μ × μ, and inflation kills the
  transgressions of Z!-invariant homomorphisms Z^{!0} → μ (Hochschild–Serre). The pages do not examine this; the margin
  of 600 only excludes splittings that agree with A on Z^{!′} or on Z^{!‴}.
